# CLAUDE.md — Anavale Wiki Project
*Auto-loaded by Claude Code at session start. Last updated: Aug 14 2026.*

**Tool roles:**
- **Claude Code** — all file edits, git operations, debugging
- **Claude Chat** — planning, strategy, review
- **DM Tools → Supabase** — all world/content/session data changes

**What lives where (never duplicate here):**
- World lore, nations, creatures, session notes → search `anavale-dm-docs` in project knowledge
- Character system spec, SCC state, stage structure → search `docs/` in project knowledge
- Code state → read the actual file before every edit

---

## 1. Every Session

**Start:**
1. Run `git log --oneline -5` and report HEAD before doing anything else.
2. Search project knowledge for any file or function before touching it.
3. If what you find in the file conflicts with project knowledge, stop and say so.
4. Never answer from memory or assumption — read the file first.

**End (standing requirement — do not wait to be asked):**
1. Append an entry to the Decision Log (date, what changed, why).
2. Update "Current Focus" below to reflect what comes next.
3. If any rule turned out to be wrong or incomplete, correct it here and commit.
4. If a new bug pattern was discovered, add it to the Decision Log.

---

## 2. Current Focus

*Session Aug 15 2026 (new session, structured investigate-then-report QA round) — `dm.html`. Environment check first, then 7 investigate-and-report items, 2 build items. Full findings in Decision Log; summary here for continuity.*
**Environment check, confirmed clean**: the local-dev banner from the prior round is real and renders correctly on a fresh server/reload — rules out "banner never built" as an explanation for any "still looks wrong" report this round.
**Dran's DM-Only box "yellow/gold" report — did not reproduce.** Expanded Dran's real NPC card live and read `getComputedStyle()` on the box border, title, and all 36 internal colored elements (icons/labels) — every single one resolves to `rgb(45,212,191)`, the correct teal, confirmed by screenshot too. No third hidden color rule exists in the current code. Most likely explanation: browser cache of an old `dm.html`/`tokens.css` on the DM's machine (an on-record recurring issue in this file), a stale local checkout, or the actual live GitHub Pages site (which has none of this branch's fixes, since this branch has never been merged to whatever branch Pages deploys from).
**Loot/shop category icons — no existing sub-categorization to build on.** `field_guide_sessions` block schema stores only a single semantic `color` field per box (`grey/green/cyan/blue/purple/orange/pink`) — there's no drink/food/general/weapon/armor/magic distinction anywhere in the data, and the block editor's box row is just a Label input + one Color dropdown. Recommended, not built: add a `subtype` field to orange (loot/shop) boxes specifically, surfaced as a small dropdown in the block editor next to the existing color select, DM-assigned per box — explicitly not auto-detected from box text (too unreliable to trust silently, per instruction).
**Roll Initiative — real behavior confirmed via `sccStartCombat()`.** It does NOT override manually-typed init values: for each combatant it reads the init input field, uses the typed value as-is if present, and only rolls a random d20+DEX for blanks — then sorts everyone (typed and rolled together) by initiative descending. So a DM who's typed every value gets a pure sort with nothing rolled; a DM who's typed none gets everyone rolled. Recommended clearer copy: "Start Combat — Sort/Roll Initiative" or a tooltip reading "Uses your typed values; rolls a d20+DEX for anything left blank, then sorts" — current copy ("Roll Initiative") overstates what happens when values are already filled in.
**Creature stat-block coverage — same 4/52 creatures for everything, confirmed field-by-field, not assumed.** Queried every field the DM listed: type/AC/CR/size/all 6 ability scores/languages/speed/senses/attacks/traits/HP are all exactly 4/52 (Grey Fluffets, Still Frogs, Grusk Enforcer, Grusk Color Extractor — same 4 every time). Deeper into those same 4: skill proficiencies 2/52, saving throws 1/52, actions 2/52, reactions 0/52, damage resistances/immunities/condition immunities all 0/52, legendary actions 0/52. No `spells` column exists at all — nothing in `attacks`/`actions`/`traits` jsonb for any creature contains spellcasting data. Weapon/damage data lives inside `attacks` jsonb (name/damage dice/type/attack bonus) — populated for the same 4. Also confirmed the combat encounter builder today only ever pulls `cr`/`hp`/`dexMod` from a creature into a combatant object — nothing else from the stat block reaches the UI at all currently, so a full stat-block display would be new UI work on top of the data gap, not just a data-entry task. Realistic picture for the DM: full-detail combat stat blocks are viable today for exactly 4 creatures; everything else needs real data entry first (AC/HP/type/size/ability scores at minimum) before any display work matters.
**Tagged Notes confusion — root-caused, not just "confusing."** Walked the actual click path: `sccAddTagNote()` posts a blank note and re-fetches, ordered `updated_at.desc`, so a new note *should* land at the very top — but two things make it feel broken. (1) If a category filter chip is currently active and the DM creates a note in a *different* category via the dropdown, the new note is created successfully in Supabase but immediately filtered out of view — the DM sees literally nothing happen. (2) Nothing auto-focuses the new note's title field or scrolls it into view or shows any success confirmation — it just silently appears (or doesn't, per #1) after a brief loading flash. Recommended: reset/match the active filter to the new note's category on create, auto-focus the title input immediately after render, and add a brief visible confirmation (toast or highlight) — not a cosmetic tweak, a real interaction-completeness fix.
**Deity NPC for QA reference, confirmed real.** `world_characters` has 5 real `category:'god'` rows — Oro, Nara, Thyun, Solvara, Grak — but relationship state (`ally/neutral/caution/hostile/deity`) is a per-session DM-toggled cycle stored in `session_notes.npc_relationships`, not tied to NPC identity; that field is currently `{}` (empty) for the one real session, so no NPC anywhere is *currently* set to "deity." Recommended for future QA: add Oro (most prominent, benevolent, widely-worshipped) to a session roster and click his relationship badge 4 times to cycle to "deity."
**Built (no investigation gate): Suggested Level range + reasoning.** New pure `sccSuggestedLevelRange(quest)` — never writes `quest.suggested_level`, only computes a display-only range + one-line reasoning shown next to the still-manually-editable field. Baseline from `first_available` ("session-N" → lvl N to N+1, modeling roughly one level per session), nudged +1 for `high` stakes / +2 for `campaign` stakes (`low`/`mid` unchanged), widened to include a related creature's CR-derived level when real CR data exists on a `related[]` creature entry (checked: today only one quest, The Greytalon, has any creature relation at all, and that creature's CR is null — so the CR nudge is real, wired, and correctly inert on all 10 current quests until CR data exists). Verified live on 3 real quests via direct function calls and the rendered UI (e.g. "Suggested range: lvl 1-2 (session 1, low stakes)" next to an untouched, still-blank "Not set" input).
**Built (no investigation gate): Combat tab layout.** Moved "+ Add All Players" into the top row next to the search box (was a separate row below the staging list). Added `margin-top:0.75rem` spacing both above and below the combatant list so it's no longer flush against the search row and the button row. "Start Combat — Roll Initiative" pushed to the far right of its row via `margin-left:auto` (Clear All stays left). Verified live with 2 real combatants staged, screenshotted, then cleared (client-side-only state, nothing to revert server-side).
Zero console errors throughout. Pre-push checks clean: raw parse, `new Function()` on all 17 script blocks, diff-scoped literal-emoji grep empty.

*Session Aug 14 2026 (new session, local-dev environment banner) — `dm.html`. Added a persistent, impossible-to-miss red banner at the very top of the page, auto-detected purely from `window.location.hostname` — no manual per-checkout setup, no build step. Shows "⚠ LOCAL DEV BUILD — NOT LIVE — {hostname}:{port}" whenever the hostname isn't exactly `carlosknightiii.github.io`; silent (no banner, zero layout shift) on the real live site. True git branch/folder name isn't detectable from a static HTML file served with no backend — confirmed this isn't achievable client-side rather than faking it, so the fallback is hostname:port exactly as the fallback the ask specified. Push-down handled via a `--dm-env-banner-h` CSS variable set from the banner's real measured height (not a hardcoded pixel guess), applied to both `.gate-overlay` (position:fixed, needs an explicit `top` override) and `.dm-app` (normal flow, needs `margin-top`) so neither the password gate nor the app header ever sits under the banner. Verified live: banner renders correctly on `localhost:PORT` with the exact right text, gate card and app header both cleanly offset below it with pixel-exact alignment (33px banner height, 33px `--dm-env-banner-h`, 33px `.dm-app` top — zero gap, zero overlap), zero console errors. Live-domain behavior verified by calling the exact same condition function used in the shipped code against `'carlosknightiii.github.io'` directly (returns `false`, correctly hidden) rather than by actually deploying, since this session works on a feature branch that was never merged to the branch GitHub Pages deploys from. One real near-miss caught by the standing pre-push check: the ⚠ warning-sign character landed as a literal glyph in the new inline script (Edit tool normalizing the escape on write, same recurring issue on record) — fixed via the established Python exact-string-replacement workaround before it ever reached a commit.*

*Session Aug 14 2026 (new session, DM's second QA pass — found real gaps in the first pass's own coverage, all investigated fresh and fixed) — `dm.html`, `css/tokens.css`. Explicit instruction not to trust prior "verified" claims; re-investigated everything from scratch.*
**Colors — the first purple/blue/orange audit genuinely wasn't exhaustive.** A fresh, complete grep of `var(--purple)`/`#c084fc` (not the narrower search used last time) turned up a 4th real usage the first pass missed entirely: `--dm-stakes-high-text: var(--purple)` — quest "high stakes" badges, reached through a token indirection (`.q-stakes-high` → `--dm-stakes-high-text` → `--purple`) the first audit's direct-usage grep didn't follow. Recommended and (confirmed) implemented: reuse `--orange-vivid` (same hex Combat's "high" difficulty already uses) rather than inventing a 10th distinct hue in an increasingly crowded palette — the two badges never appear in the same view, so sharing a hue for the same underlying idea ("elevated severity") is a feature, not a collision. The reported DM-Only-vs-Vareth collision **did not reproduce** — investigated every real Vareth-related visual element (the location-card VARETH badge is a plain grey `#a0a0b0`, unrelated to teal; two `.scc-flag--vareth`/`.scc-flag--formery` CSS rules exist but are dead code, never referenced by any HTML/JS) and found no actual overlap; reported this back rather than inventing a fix for a collision that isn't there.
**A much bigger gap: two entire red "DM-only" surfaces the first pass never found.** Grepping the actual red hex (`#d62b2b`, `#f87171`) instead of relying on memory of what had already been fixed turned up: (1) `.scc-npc2-dm-block` — the large "DM Only — Do Not Read Aloud" card on NPC/location panel cards, with its nested "Secret"/"Notes"/"Vareth Presence" row labels (`.scc-npc2-red`) — a completely separate rendering surface (NPC/Location panel expanded cards, not the lookup drawer) that neither of the two earlier DM-Only recolor passes had touched, still hardcoded to `#d62b2b`. (2) Quest "Reveals" boxes (`.quest-reveal-item`, explicitly labeled "DM eyes only" in its own header) — also still on the pre-recolor `rgba(248,113,113,...)` red. Both converted to the teal system. Separately, `.scc-dm-text` (the body-text rule inside the `.scc-dm-block` box fixed last round) turned out to still hardcode `color: var(--dm-red)` — the earlier fix only touched the shared `--scc-dm-only-*` token set for background/border/label, never noticing this one rule bypassed that token set entirely — exactly explaining the reported "teal border, red/brown body text" inconsistency. Fixed by pointing it at `--scc-dm-only-text` (a token that already existed, previously unused).
**Suggested Level**: confirmed genuinely interactive (real `<input type="number">`, not disabled/readonly, exposed correctly as an accessible textbox) — the "looks non-interactive" read was about visual affordance, not function. Added a dashed border while empty (`:placeholder-shown`) plus a hover cue, so an unset field visibly invites a click instead of reading as static label text.
**Field Guide bleeding into every Session sub-tab — a real, confirmed CSS bug, not intentional.** `#scc-panel-fieldguide { display: flex; ... }` was a bare ID rule with higher specificity than the base `.scc-panel { display: none; }`, so it stayed visually present (search bar, filter chips, permanently-stuck "Loading Field Guide…") underneath every other Session sub-panel regardless of which one was actually `.active`. Gated the rule on `.active` like every other panel; confirmed both that the leak is gone on Quests/Combat/Notes and that Field Guide itself still renders correctly (grid layout, real document content) when it is the active tab.
**Combat roster quick-add chips**: confirmed they hard-disabled after one click (`SCC_COMBATANTS.some(id match)` guard, plus the render logic swapping to a permanently non-interactive `<span>`) — unlike the search box, which the previous round fixed. Made the chips stay clickable indefinitely, replacing the old checkmark-and-disable with a live `×N` count, so the DM keeps the "already used" signal without losing the ability to add more.
**Tagged Notes**: moved the whole section (hint text, add-row, search/filters, list) to sit between the 5 structured recap fields and the Save/Copy/Session-Completed controls — during-session content before end-of-session actions, not after. Split the cramped single-row toolbar into a visually distinct gold-boxed "add a note" row (category select + primary-styled "+ New Note" button) sitting above a plainer search/filter row, so adding a note reads as the section's primary action instead of one more filter control.
Verified every fix live (screenshots + computed-style checks, not assumed), zero console errors, all test data cleaned up. Pre-push checks clean — the only emoji hits in the diff were 3 pre-existing characters relocated by the Tagged Notes reposition (confirmed present in the prior commit via `git show HEAD:dm.html`, not newly introduced).*

*Session Aug 14 2026 (new session, DM QA pass — 3 color collisions investigated + fixed, 6 bugs investigated + fixed, 2 combat features built) — `dm.html`, `css/tokens.css`, `field_guide_sessions` data. Full findings + all fixes in Decision Log; summary here for continuity.*
**Colors (investigated fresh, reported, confirmed before touching hex):** purple's 3-way collision (deity/Combat-high/Field Guide comedy, all literally `#c084fc`) resolved by keeping purple for deity (rarest, best semantic fit) and moving Combat-high to vivid orange `#fb923c` (freed up by the loot/shop change below — gives a real green→gold→orange→red difficulty ramp) and Field Guide comedy to pink `#f472b6` (a hue with zero prior semantic meaning). DM-Only's cyan — now confirmed reading as "blue" for the *second* time against Field Guide lore boxes and the NPC "neutral" badge — moved to a true teal (`--cyan` token redefined to `#2dd4bf`) *and* paired with a 💠 icon added to every player_facing "DM Only" text badge (character/organization/region/religion/species), not just Field Guide boxes, since hue alone had already failed once. Loot/shop's alarm-toned orange desaturated to a terracotta `#d9a66c` (kept the hue family rather than switching, per the DM's own instinct minus gold, which is already overloaded 5+ ways) — a 🪙 coin icon was added to loot/shop box labels unconditionally, per instruction, regardless of the color outcome.

Mira Pont's/Goldenway's missing Shop sections were confirmed to be the vendor_stock cleanup from the original build's own verification pass (0 rows for everyone) — the section and its add-stock search box both render and work correctly right now; no code bug found or needed. Nations were reachable via global search but mislabeled "Type: Location" (a real, previously-flagged-but-unfixed bug in the city/POI/nation lookup branch's `_srcType` check) and missing nation-specific fields entirely — fixed the type-label logic to a reliable direct-array-membership check and added Government/Capital/Beliefs/Gigglegloom Relationship fields. Item (and Quest/Creature/Organization) DM Notes/Secret blocks were still rendering in the pre-recolor red — the shared `--scc-dm-only-*` token set was never touched by the original cyan recolor pass (only the separate badge color was) — fixed once at the token level, fixing all 6 call sites simultaneously. Manage tab name truncation was a real CSS bug: 3 stacked hardcoded pixel caps (220px/240px duplicate `.cm-td-name` rules plus a 180px `.cm-name-link` cap) truncating names regardless of available space — removed all three, also cleaning up the genuine duplicate rule this file's own conventions forbid.
**Features:** Combat's search-based creature add was silently blocked from adding the same creature twice (`sccPickerSelect`'s dedup guard, meant for the other 4 picker types, had no real purpose for combatants — `SCC_COMBATANTS` is array-indexed everywhere, never looked up by id) — carved out a combatant-specific bypass so 3x Grusk Enforcer works via repeated search-and-click. Added a "Clear All" button next to Add All Players, confirm-gated, wired into the same show/hide logic as the other encounter-builder buttons.
Verified live end-to-end for every item (see Decision Log), zero console errors, all test data (vendor_stock rows, combat staging) cleaned up afterward. Pre-push checks clean — one real near-miss caught and fixed before it shipped: the DM-Only icon edit initially landed as a literal emoji character in 5 JS string literals (Edit tool normalized the `💠` escape into the literal glyph on write) — caught by the standard post-edit emoji grep, fixed via a Python script for exact byte-level replacement, per this file's own established C2 workaround.*

*Session Aug 14 2026 (same session as the investigation below — DM confirmed the block-based storage shape and the manual `[[type:id|Label]]` link syntax — built and shipped) — `dm.html`, new `field_guide_sessions` table. This closes out the last remaining goal from the Aug 12 2026 DM Tools UX/IA investigation. New `field_guide_sessions` table (`session_number unique`, `blocks jsonb`, RLS mirroring the anon-all-access pattern), replacing the fully static 505-line hardcoded Session 1 document with a data-driven renderer. Session 1's real content was parsed out of the old hardcoded HTML with a one-off Python/BeautifulSoup script (scratch-only, not committed) into 140 ordered blocks — 48 boxes, 30 h2, 20 standalone tables (2 more genuinely nested inside orange shop boxes, matching the investigation's own finding), 14 h1, 15 p, 4 italic, 4 lists, plus title/subtitle/tagline/meta/footer — and inserted as one row via direct SQL. **Verification was rigorous, not visual**: fetched the real *pre-migration* `dm.html` (via `git show HEAD:dm.html`) into a hidden fragment, extracted its `textContent`, extracted the new block-rendered version's `textContent` the same way, and confirmed they're identical once whitespace is stripped entirely (34,733 non-whitespace characters, exact match) — the only difference was insignificant block-boundary spacing from the old source's manual indentation, not content. New `sccFgRenderBlock()`/`sccFgRenderDoc()` reconstruct the exact same CSS classes the static markup used (`scc-fg-doc-h1`, `scc-fg-box scc-fg-box--{color}`, `scc-fg-table` + variants, etc.) so zero CSS changed. A new session selector (`<select>` sourced from the already-loaded `SCC_ALL_SESSIONS` — no new fetch needed) sits above the document; switching it also fixes the real pre-existing bug the investigation found — `SCC_FG_DATA` (the sidebar's session-scoped NPC/creature/location/item cache) is now explicitly reset to `null` on every session switch, so the sidebar actually refetches for the newly selected session instead of staying stuck on whichever session loaded first. A DM-facing editor groups blocks by their nearest preceding `h1` into collapsible sections (same shape as tonight's `session_scenarios` editor, but with a richer per-row type set: text inputs for headings, HTML-capable textareas for prose, and genuinely structured row/header editors for tables — including the 2 real box-nested-table cases — rather than raw-HTML editing anywhere). Inline entity links use manual `[[type:id|Label]]` markup (per the DM's confirmed choice over auto-detection), parsed at render time into a span wired to the existing `sccOpenLookup()` drawer — paired with a link-insert helper reusing the `.scc-picker-search`/`.scc-picker-dropdown` UI already established this session, so the DM finds the right entity by name instead of typing raw ids. Verified live end-to-end: the byte-for-byte content check above; a real link inserted via the actual search-and-click UI (linked "Pellam Voss" text to the real NPC), saved, and clicked in read view — correctly opened the real lookup drawer showing Pellam Voss's full card; the empty-session state (`SCC_FG_DOC_SESSION_NUM` pointed at a session with no row) correctly rendered "No Field Guide written for Session 2 yet" instead of erroring; the session selector correctly shows just "Session 1" today with no crash. All test edits (the inserted link) reverted afterward, confirmed via SQL the real document is back to its exact 140-block migrated state. Zero console errors throughout. Pre-push checks clean (raw parse, `new Function()` on all 16 script blocks, diff-scoped literal-emoji grep empty — the box labels' real emoji characters, e.g. 💠, are Supabase data fetched at runtime, not literal characters in `dm.html`'s own JS source, so C2 doesn't apply to them).*

*Session Aug 13 2026 (new session, Field Guide multi-session support — investigation + 2 open design questions, no code changed) — confirmed the current Session 1 Field Guide document (`dm.html` ~3236-3740) is a single, fully hardcoded 505-line HTML block with real richness: 14 numbered `scc-fg-doc-h1` sections, 30 `scc-fg-doc-h2` subsections, 48 colored callout boxes across 6 CSS color variants (green/yellow(renders cyan)/blue/purple/orange/grey), 22 tables (2 genuinely nested inside orange "shop" boxes — Mira Pont's stock, the Inn's drinks — the rest standalone 2-3 column key/value or scenario tables), 4 bullet lists, plus document-level title/subtitle/tagline/meta/footer text. The color legend is itself a real, migratable sentence inside the grey "HOW TO USE THIS GUIDE" box — not app chrome, so it isn't being hardcoded separately, it moves with Session 1's content like everything else. Confirmed the sidebar's session-scoped fetch (`sccRenderFieldGuide()`, keys off `SCC_SESSION.session_number` to filter NPCs/creatures/locations/items down to what's assigned in Setup) is real and reusable, parameterizable to any session number with a small change — but it's completely disconnected from the static document today, and a real pre-existing bug was found in the process: `SCC_FG_DATA` is cached forever and never reset to `null` anywhere, so the sidebar wouldn't actually refresh even if something did point it at a different session. Recommended storage: a new `field_guide_sessions` table, one row per `session_number`, holding the entire document as one ordered `blocks` jsonb array (title/subtitle/tagline/meta/h1/h2/italic/p/list/table/box/footer block types) rather than a handful of named columns — reasoning: the document's real content is too heterogeneous (arbitrary mixes of prose, boxes, and nested tables per section) for a clean typed-column schema, and a flat ordered block list is both the safest way to guarantee lossless migration (each block stores its real inner HTML/text close to verbatim) and a natural fit for a collapsible-by-h1-section editor mirroring tonight's `session_scenarios` pattern. Tables get genuine structured sub-fields (header row + data rows, both top-level and nested inside a box) rather than raw HTML, since they're a large, editable-worthy fraction of the real content (NPC reference cards, price lists). One open design question sent for confirmation: inline entity links — manual `[[type:id|Label]]` markup (DM-controlled, paired with a searchable link-insert helper so the DM doesn't need to memorize IDs) recommended over auto-detecting entity names in prose text, since auto-linking risks false positives on short, common-word-adjacent real names in this exact dataset (Gerald, Grey, Vel, Fess, Mira) and this app has consistently favored explicit DM-authored links (`quest.related[]`) over inferred ones everywhere else. Waiting on confirmation of the storage shape and the link mechanism before writing any schema or UI code, per instruction. Full findings in Decision Log.*

Also replicated the "browse with an empty query once a filter is active" behavior the global search got 2 sessions ago — a DM can now see e.g. "every Tier: humanoid creature" with nothing typed. Zero console errors, zero Supabase writes needed cleanup (combat staging is pure client-side state until "Start Combat," unchanged by this pass). Pre-push checks clean (raw parse, `new Function()` on all 16 script blocks, diff-scoped literal-emoji grep empty).*

*Session Aug 13 2026 (new session, Combat Tracker filters — investigation only, no code changed, waiting on go-ahead for a recommended filter set) — confirmed the CR sparsity is real and unchanged (4/52 creatures), and confirmed it's the *same* 4 creatures that have any real stat-block data at all (`ac`/`hp_max`/`speed_ft`/`creature_type`/`size`/`creature_alignment`/`xp_value` are all also exactly 4/52 — Grey Fluffets, Still Frogs, Grusk Enforcer, Grusk Color Extractor — everything else is a lore-only entry with no D&D mechanics). Recommended CR as a **sort** (Name/CR toggle, no-CR creatures sort last), not a filter — a CR dropdown would only ever bucket the same 4 creatures into 3 values, no real utility. Bigger finding: `tier` and `category` (already flagged as usable) are genuinely 52/52 populated with clean, well-distributed enums (tier: 11 values, common 21 down to unseen/unknown 1 each; category: 12 values, region-exclusive 9 down to the-unseen 1) — real, working dropdown filters, just never wired into the Combat tab's own `sccPickerSearch('combatant', ...)` box (they're already exposed on the *global* header search from 2 sessions ago, a completely separate UI surface — this pass is additive, not a duplicate). `regions[]` (array, 52/52 populated) resolves to exactly the 4 real region ids (caparia/nombi/sohot/jugabi) confirmed against the `regions` table, each creature tagged to 2–3 on average — confirmed as a genuinely usable "find creatures near here" filter, and confirmed as the correct substitute for "nation": nations belong to regions (15 real nations, each pinned to exactly one of the 4 regions via `nations.region`), but creatures carry zero nation-level tagging, only the coarser region level — building a nation filter would need new schema, building a region filter doesn't. `habitat` is also 52/52 populated but every single value is unique free-text lore prose (52 distinct values, confirmed via a full group-by) — useless as a dropdown, but real material to fold into the search box's own text match (currently name-only) alongside `tags`/`description`, which would make typing e.g. "rainforest" or "vareth" actually surface something without any new UI at all. `tags[]` (52/52 populated) is real but overlaps heavily with tier/category plus a long tail of one-off flavor tags (`omen`, `prophetic`, `wanderkeep-indicator`) — not clean enough for its own dropdown, but same free-text-search value as habitat. Confirmed (not re-derived from the task's own framing) that `world_characters` has zero HP/AC/CR-shaped columns — NPCs-in-combat is out of scope this pass per instruction, not touched. Recommended filter set sent for go-ahead: Tier dropdown, Category dropdown, Region dropdown, a Name/CR sort toggle, and widening the existing search box to match habitat/description/tags text — nothing invented for army/nation/race/level, since none of those exist as real, populated, filter-worthy data today. Full findings in Decision Log.*

*Session Aug 13 2026 (same investigation thread as below, DM answered all 4 design questions — fixed per-vendor list, no new vendor flag, buy-only, woven into the lookup drawer — built and shipped) — `dm.html`, new `vendor_stock` table. Scoped exactly to the 4 confirmed decisions, nothing more. New `vendor_stock` table (`entity_collection`/`entity_id`/`item_id`/`price_gp_override`/`quantity`/`notes`/`sort_order`), RLS mirroring `session_note_entries`' anon-all-access pattern, `updated_at` trigger reusing that same table's existing `update_session_notes_timestamp()` function rather than writing a duplicate. No new flag on `pois`/`organizations` — vendor-hood is purely "has rows in `vendor_stock`," per the DM's own confirmed answer to question 2. `sccInit()`'s existing `Promise.all` gained one more `sccFetch('vendor_stock', ...)` call into a new `window.VENDOR_STOCK` global, same pattern as `species`/`items`/`conditions`. New `sccVendorShopSectionHtml(collection, entityId)` renders a "Shop" section — shared buyer `<select>` (real `SCC_PLAYERS` roster), one row per stocked item (thumbnail, name, catalog price shown alongside an editable price-override input and an editable quantity input — both blank = "use catalog price"/"unlimited," matching the confirmed fixed-list-with-overrides design), a qty-to-buy input + Buy button (hidden, replaced with "Out of stock"/"No price set"/"No players" when purchase isn't currently possible), and a search-to-add-stock box reusing the exact `.scc-picker-search`/`.scc-picker-dropdown`/`.scc-picker-option` markup/CSS `sccLootItemSearch()` already uses — zero new dropdown CSS needed. Wired into `sccOpenLookup()`'s existing `city` branch (line ~13817) and `organization` branch (line ~13968), appended after each branch's existing Tags section. **Real gating bug caught before shipping, not after**: the `city` branch's own `loc` variable is pulled from a fresh `CITIES.concat(POIS).concat(NATIONS)` built inline in that function — it does **not** carry the `_srcType` tag a separate, unrelated function attaches to its own independently-built array elsewhere in the file (confirmed via grep before trusting it), so `loc._srcType === 'poi'` — the check the branch's own pre-existing (and, it turns out, already-silently-broken) "POI" type-label logic relies on — would never actually be true here. Used a direct, reliable check instead: `POIS.some(function(p){ return p.id === loc.id; })`, scoping the Shop section strictly to real POI rows and correctly excluding cities/nations that happen to render through the same branch. Buy transaction (`sccVendorBuy()`) mirrors `sccAwardCoins()`'s gold-arithmetic pattern and `sccAwardItem()`'s inventory-insert shape exactly, composed together with an affordability check (blocks and toasts if the player's real `gold_cp` can't cover it, zero writes) and a stock-quantity check (blocks if buying more than what's left) before either `sccPatch`ing gold down or `sccPost`ing the real inventory row (`dm_granted:false`, since this is a real purchase, not a DM grant) — decrements `vendor_stock.quantity` afterward only when it isn't null (unlimited stock never depletes). Add/remove/edit-stock and the buy transaction all end by re-invoking `sccOpenLookup()` on the same entity so the drawer reflects the fresh state immediately, matching the re-render-in-place pattern this file already uses elsewhere (tag notes, scenario editor). No sell-back path built at all, per the confirmed buy-only scope. Verified live end-to-end against real Supabase data via a fresh local server (a stale server was already running on the old dev port from an earlier session — started a new one on a new port and confirmed via `curl` before trusting it, per the standing lesson): opened Mira Pont's Sundry Goods POI (the same entity whose informal `"shop"` tag drove the original investigation) through the real search-and-click UI, added a real Torch to its stock via the real search box, confirmed the row landed in Supabase with `price_gp_override:null, quantity:null` (catalog price, unlimited). Bought 1 as Hana Sable through the real Buy button — confirmed via SQL her `gold_cp` dropped 900→899 (1cp, matching Torch's real 0.01gp price) and a real `inventory` row was inserted. Set a deliberately unaffordable price override (10000gp) and clicked Buy again — confirmed via SQL zero writes happened (gold and inventory both unchanged), toast correctly blocked it. Set quantity to 0 — confirmed the row correctly flipped to "Out of stock" with the Buy control hidden. Opened a real Organization (Goldenway) and confirmed its own independent Shop section renders correctly scoped to `entity_collection:'organizations'`. Opened a real City (Gobblewump Crossing, confirmed via `POIS.some()` that it is NOT a real POI) and confirmed the Shop section correctly does not render — the POI-scoping fix holds. Zero console errors throughout. All test data (the stock row, the inventory row, Hana's gold) reverted via SQL afterward, confirmed clean via a final read. Pre-push checks clean (raw parse, `new Function()` on all 16 extracted script blocks, diff-scoped literal-emoji grep empty).*

*Session Aug 12 2026 (new session, Vendor/Shop — investigation only, no code changed, waiting on 4 real design questions) — confirmed fully net-new as expected: zero vendor/shop flag or field anywhere on `pois`/`organizations` (`poi_type` real values are town/dark-zone/site/landmark/waystation — nothing shop-like), Mira Pont's real shop stock exists only as hardcoded HTML prose in the Field Guide document, not linked to the real `items` catalog at all. One soft signal found: her POI row already carries a free-text `"shop"` tag in its `tags` array — informal, not structured, but a real existing DM convention worth reusing rather than inventing a parallel one. Confirmed `items.cost_gp` (127/139 non-null, 18 of those are genuinely 0 — mostly cosmetic accessory-slot rows from the appearance-catalog migration, not shop-worthy — and 12 carry real fractional PHB-accurate prices like 0.01/0.05/0.5 gp) and `character_sheet.gold_cp` (integer copper-pieces, `renderGold()`/`onCurrencyBlur()` in `sheet/index.html`) as the two real quantities a purchase would need to reconcile. Bigger finding than expected: the low-level transaction mechanics already exist in `dm.html`, just never composed into a priced flow — `sccAwardCoins()` already does `character_sheet.gold_cp` arithmetic via `sccPatch()`, `sccAwardItem()` already POSTs a real `inventory` row (`character_id, token, item_id, quantity, dm_granted`) — a "buy" action is these same two calls with a cost check and subtraction instead of addition, not new plumbing. Neither call is wrapped in a real DB transaction (two independent REST calls) — a pre-existing pattern, not a new risk this would introduce, but worth having agreed with the DM going in. `sheet/index.html`'s `runInventorySearch()`/`selectCatalogItem()` (the +Add Item modal) is the closest reusable browse-and-buy UI pattern — name+category search against `items`, qty input, one-click add. 4 real design questions sent to the DM (stock model, whether a new vendor flag is needed, buy-only vs. buy+sell, and UI placement) — waiting on answers before writing any schema or code, per instruction. Full findings in Decision Log.*

*Session Aug 12 2026 (new session, closing out the residual inconsistency flagged at the end of the prior session) — `dm.html`: swapped the Field Guide's hardcoded DM-Only label glyph from `&#x1F7E1;` (🟡 yellow circle — a leftover from before that box was recolored cyan) to `&#x1F4A0;` (💠 diamond), all 18 real occurrences (confirmed via grep every single one sits directly inside a `.scc-fg-box--yellow` box, no exceptions, no unrelated usage). No cyan circle emoji exists in Unicode, so a genuinely different glyph was needed rather than a like-for-like color swap — 💠 was picked since it reads as a status/callout marker the same way the old circle did, without implying a specific wrong color. Verified via `git stash` before/after (🟡 in a cyan box → 💠 in a cyan box) and `codePointAt()` confirming the live DOM text is exactly U+1F4A0, not a mis-encoded fallback glyph. Pre-push checks clean (syntax, no literal emoji introduced, only entities). Committed and pushed to this branch (`anavale-wiki-scc-ux`).*

Flagged, not changed: the real Figma frame shows a Temp HP bar/max too, but that contradicts an explicit DM decision earlier this same session to keep Temp HP as a plain badge (5e has no real max temp HP) — left as-is pending a DM call on which one wins. Also flagged: "Noto Sans Runic" (named in the ask) isn't declared anywhere in the codebase; the rune glyphs render fine via browser font fallback, but there's nothing to "fix" since it was never wired in.
- **Ground-truth Figma rebuild, same session, after the cosmetic pass below** (`sheet/index.html`, `css/tokens.css`): DM provided exact pulled Figma values (not descriptions) across 7 areas and asked for item-by-item verification against the live page before any code — several items turned out to already be correct (condition order, identity pills, Your Background structure/title size), several were more broken than described, and a few needed DM decisions before implementing (documented below). Committed as one unit (`f2075fa`) after two rounds of DM confirmation.
  New tokens: `--blue-50/100/400/600/800`, `--char-ov-h5` (18px).
  **Header**: added the "Character Sheet" page title (logo + text) — confirmed missing entirely, not a restyle. Removed the header's weight bar (not in Figma; Inventory tab already had its own independent "Carrying: X lb" line, so nothing was lost) — deleted `renderOverviewWeight()` and its markup/CSS as dead code. AC badge rebuilt to exact values (blue/100 bg, blue/800 text, bottom-only 55px radius, split "AC" label/number typography) — was a single uniform gold pill. HP row rebuilt: current AND max are both real `<input type="number">` fields, no Edit toggle, no −/+/Apply/Set-Max-Mod panel — editing max derives `hp_max_modifier` from the typed value minus the real `hp_max` base (DM's explicit call, replacing the separate Max-Mod button rather than adding a third field). Temp HP gets the same direct-input treatment and **no longer hides at 0** — a deliberate reversal of the earlier "hides entirely at 0/null" decision, since a directly-typeable field needs to always be present to type into; flagged to the DM as an implied consequence of switching interaction models, not silently changed.
  **Who You Are**: confirmed the exact bug the DM described — `storyCard()` was shared by all three Your Story sections, so the 5(6) personality items rendered as separate `blue-700`/rounded-24px boxes, visually identical to Your Background/Your Past cards. Pulled a fresh high-res Figma crop mid-session (not relying on the earlier lower-res pull) and rebuilt into the real structure: one `cs-whoyouare-card` container with a restored "Summary" heading (stripped in an earlier pass when fixing the six-section-dump bug) + paragraph, then each item as a flush `border-left:4px solid blue-400` strip with no radius/own background. Eyebrow+title text for 5 of 6 items transcribed verbatim off that crop (not paraphrased) — First Impression/People Instantly Notice About You, Misunderstood/People Get Wrong About You, Primary Care/Care About, Fear/Deepest Fear, Quest/What You Seek. The 6th field (`personality_laugh`) isn't in that Figma frame at all — flagged to the DM rather than inventing copy; DM supplied eyebrow "Laugh" / title "What Always Makes You Laugh" directly, confirmed landing verbatim (not a paraphrase) before committing. Appearance chips section removed (redundant with the header's Appearance paragraph, confirmed still present before deleting).
  **Eyebrow styling sitewide**: `.cs-story-card-label` (shared by Who You Are/Your Background/Your Past — one class, one fix) changed from Bold/gold/0.68rem to Light 400/`--blue-100`/12px, matching the real Figma value exactly.
  **Your Background / Your Past icons**: bumped the shared `.cs-story-card-img` 48px → 75px. Your Background's card structure and 26px title size were already correct — verified before touching, not assumed broken just because Your Past was.
  **Your Past cards**: rebuilt as a new dedicated `pastCard()` (distinct from `storyCard()`, which stays as Your Background's builder) — fixed 358px width (confirmed via computed style, not eyeballed) and 18px titles via new `--char-ov-h5` (Your Background stays 26px). Real structural split confirmed against the Figma frame: 5 cards (Upbringing/Friend/Pet/Love/Organization) keep the icon beside only the header row; Left Behind/Why You Left use a `--full` row-layout variant with the icon beside the *entire* content column — previously all 7 used the same header-only layout, a genuine mismatch, not just a styling gap. Also caught mid-rebuild: none of the 7 Your Past cards had a real title line distinct from the eyebrow (one label was doing double duty) — added verbatim eyebrow+title pairs for all 7 (e.g. eyebrow "Upbringing" / title "Who Raised You"), sourced from the same Figma crop; corrected "Why Your Left" (a Figma-mockup typo) to the grammatically correct "Why You Left" already used elsewhere in the file rather than replicating the typo.
  **Benefits box**: rebuilt from rounded pill-tags (`cs-story-benefit-tag`) into a typed-line `blue-600` box (confirmed via zoomed Figma crop, not guessed) shared by Your Background's Background card and the relevant Your Past cards — ability deltas combine onto one comma-joined line ("+2 WIS, +1 STR", no suffix/tooltip), skill lines get an optional amount + "(Skill Proficiency)" + tooltip, feats get "(Ability)" + tooltip, DM-add items get "(DM adds)" with no tooltip, and a new situational-description type gets "(Situational)" with no tooltip and non-bold body text. Building Had a Pet's situational line required a new `PET_EFFECT` lookup (DM-approved) sourced verbatim from `character.html`'s `data-effect` attributes on the had_pet option cards, same pattern as the existing `WHY_LEFT_QUEST` — confirmed live (not just by reading the code) that an unrecognized `had_pet` value degrades to no Benefits box and no literal "undefined", by calling `buildYourPastHtml()` directly in the browser console with a fake value before reporting it fixed.
  **Tab bar**: rebuilt from underline tabs (uppercase, gold text+underline) to pill tabs — active `blue-400` background at 100% opacity, inactive `blue-600` at 50% opacity, rounded top corners, full-width `blue-400` bottom border, Roboto SemiBold 16px — confirmed via `getComputedStyle()` reading back the exact resolved colors/opacity, not just visual comparison.
  **Notes tab**: added as a real 5th tab (`TABS` array), existing Notes UI relocated verbatim/unstyled — same low-risk relocation pattern as the earlier Gold/Equipped/Inventory move.
  **Accordion animation**: added a `grid-template-rows: 1fr → 0fr` transition (~220ms ease) wrapping the existing `cs-ov-acc-body` in a new `cs-ov-acc-body-outer` grid container — no JS height measurement, works for any content height. `toggleSheetAccordion()`'s logic (a plain class toggle) was untouched and needed no changes.
  Verification: full page checked on Kael Evander and a second full pass on Visenya Oryn (different gigglegloom/region/background/alignment/past-answer combinations — including a coincidentally-shared `had_pet` value confirming the new lookup isn't accidentally Kael-specific), zero console errors on both across all 5 tabs. Screenshots via a tall-viewport fresh-navigate pattern (a scroll-then-screenshot approach hit the same Browser-pane rendering glitch noted in the Aug 9 Class-tab entry below — worked around the same way, not re-investigated). One environment note, not a code issue: the local dev server's `tokens.css` response kept getting served stale by the browser cache after edits mid-session — worked around per-check with a manual cache-busting query string on the `<link>` href; flagged to the DM in case it affects their own local testing.
- **Cosmetic/structural pass against the real Figma mockup, same session, after the tab-cleanup pass** (`sheet/index.html`): 7 items requested together, then 3 follow-up corrections after DM review, all committed together as one unit (`d2572ad`). Header AC/HP/Temp HP rebuilt from AC-alone-on-its-own-row + HP's always-visible raw −/+/Apply/Max-Mod controls + a Temp HP plain-text link, into one compact row (AC badge, HP badge+bar, Temp HP badge) — every editing control preserved, just moved behind small "Edit" toggles into a shared panel below the row (an inline-per-item version reflowed the AC badge unpredictably when a panel opened; moving both panels out fixed it). "YOUR STORY" page title added above Who You Are, then to Ability Scores/Inventory/Class too once confirmed none of the four tabs had one (contrary to the initial ask's premise) — one shared `pageTitleHtml()` helper. Icon thumbnails added to all 7 Your Past cards and 3 of 4 Your Background cards (Gigglegloom Affinity/Home Region/Background — not Moral Alignment, which has no image anywhere in the codebase to source from, confirmed by reading `character.html`, not fabricated), all verbatim from the exact asset paths `character.js`/`character.html` already use for these same option cards. Skill-proficiency benefit tags got `?` tooltips via new `SKILL_TIPS` (mirrored from `character.js`). Identity pills row removed (redundant with the blurb, not in Figma) along with the now-dead `SPECIES_PHB` lookup. Condition pills reordered to render after "+ Add Condition" per the mockup. Moral Alignment card fixed to pull from a new `ALIGNMENT_CARD_DESC` (real second-person text verbatim from `character.html`'s `.char-alignment-desc`) instead of the third-person `ALIGNMENT_TRAIT_SENTENCE` meant for the header blurb — wrong source, not a wording problem. **DM-reviewed follow-up**: Temp HP had briefly grown a decorative always-100%-filled bar (added to visually match the new AC/HP compact badges) — reverted to a plain number badge on DM instruction, since 5e has no real "max temp HP" to show a bar or proportion against; still correctly hides entirely at 0/null. Verified live on Kael and Visenya (different gigglegloom/region/background/alignment combinations each), zero console errors, before/after via `git stash`.
- **Structural cleanup pass, same session, after the tab shell landed** (`sheet/index.html`): four fixes requested together, all low-risk relocations/bug fixes, no redesign. (1) Gold/Equipped/Inventory/Starting Gear were still rendering inside Your Story — leftover from before the tab redesign, never relocated when Inventory got its own tab. Moved all four to the Inventory tab verbatim (same ids, same `renderGold()`/`renderEquipped()`/`renderInventory()` calls) so Inventory has real working content instead of a placeholder. (2) Proficient Skills moved from Your Past to Ability Scores — its real conceptual home, flagged as homeless when it was first built. (3) `buildSummaryCardHtml()` was dumping all six `generateSummary()` sections (Who Are You/Your Magic/Your Background/Your Past/Your Appearance/Your Compass) into the Who You Are card — confirmed via a stash/screenshot before-comparison. Fixed to show only the "WHO ARE YOU" section via the existing `extractSummarySection()` helper; the other five are already covered by their own dedicated cards. (4) Appearance chips moved from between the Your Background/Your Past accordions to directly after Who You Are, so it groups with the rest of the who-you-are content instead of floating between two unrelated accordions — exact placement flagged as DM's call to adjust after seeing it live. **Follow-up, DM-requested after reviewing the above**: the portrait card's Copper/Silver/Gold split-entry boxes (`renderOverviewMoney()`/`onOverviewMoneyChange()`) turned out to be a second live editor for the exact same `character_sheet.gold_cp` field the new Inventory-tab Gold section (`renderGold()`/`setGold()`) already owns — confirmed by reading both functions, not assumed. They were built to stay in sync when both lived on the same page (Aug 7 session); now that Gold has its own tab, keeping two editors for one field was pure duplication, not two different features. Removed the header boxes entirely (markup, both functions, both call sites, the now-dead `cs-ov-money-*` CSS) — `renderGold()`/`setGold()` in the Inventory tab remain the single editor. Left the now-unused `--currency-copper/silver/gold-coin` tokens in `tokens.css` (not asked, and tokens.css tolerates defined-but-unused tokens without harm). Verified end-to-end on Kael (before/after via `git stash`) and spot-checked Visenya's old-format-summary fallback still degrades to no card, both with zero console errors.
- **Hana and Visenya's `player_characters.summary` are still old-format** (confirmed via SQL: both 186 chars with no "YOUR APPEARANCE" section; Kael's is 1530 chars with all six sections — Kael's was regenerated post-rewrite, theirs weren't). This matters now because the header's appearance paragraph (below) reads from this field: Hana and Visenya will show the `buildAppearanceDesc(app)` fallback style (choppier, fragment-based — "Tall, lean build. Adult. Heart face...") instead of Kael's flowing generateSummary() prose until their summaries are regenerated. Not fixed — DM's explicit call to leave it on record rather than act on it now.
- **"Your Past" label maps rebuilt in full** (`sheet/index.html`) — all 5 maps flagged as stale last check-in (`RAISED_LABELS`, `PET_LABELS`, `LOVE_LABELS`, `ORG_LABELS`, `LEFT_BEHIND_LABELS`) now resolve every real `character.html` option value to its exact authored card text, not sheet's own earlier abbreviated guesses or the cap+dash-replace fallback. New `FRIEND_LABELS` added — `dearest_friend` never had a map at all before, always rendered the raw stored slug. All dead/unused keys dropped (26 of them, combined, across the old 5 maps). Verified before/after on Kael and Visenya via live DOM reads before committing, not assumed from the diff.
- **Header appearance paragraph fixed** (`sheet/index.html`) — was showing `appearance_prompt` verbatim (the raw AI-portrait-generation prompt string, e.g. "Fantasy portrait of, average height, athletic, adult, with #ffba66 skin..."), now shows the "YOUR APPEARANCE" section extracted from the same `generateSummary()` text already used in Who You Are (new `extractSummarySection()`, built on a factored-out `parseSummaryBlocks()` shared with `buildSummaryCardHtml()` so the two can't drift). Falls back to `buildAppearanceDesc(app)` when a character's summary predates the six-section rewrite (see the Hana/Visenya bullet above). Of the three original appearance-duplication copies (header paragraph / chips / Who You Are narrative), this actually reduces the duplication rather than just relocating it: the header and Who You Are now show the *same* text (when a character has a new-format summary) instead of two independently-worded retellings — down to two distinct versions (that shared text, plus the chips) from three.
- **`dm.html` Combat tab "Add Condition" modal** (`sccPromptCondition()`, third and last instance of the hardcoded-conditions gap): had its own separate local `COND_DATA` — 15 core conditions only, no Muted/Fading Stage 1/Color-Drained/Exhaustion, never referenced `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA` even after the Live Session panel fix earlier this session. Replaced entirely with `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA` (already fetched once in `sccInit()`) — the modal now lists all 18 real conditions with real colors/tooltips. No local fallback data left in this function at all, unlike the Live Session panel (which still has `COND_DATA` as a legacy fast-path plus this table as the fallback) — this modal only ever had the one data source to begin with, so full replacement was the right call, not a hybrid.
- **Inspiration badge tooltip + tooltip viewport clamping** (`sheet/index.html`): the Inspiration badge never got a `data-tip` — added the DM's exact rules text ("Spend before any d20 roll — roll twice, take the higher result. You can only hold one at a time.") and a `wireAllTooltips()` call inside `renderInspiration()` itself (it wasn't calling it before; relied on the caller to, which `toggleInspiration()` didn't). Testing this surfaced a real, previously-invisible bug in the shared `wireTooltip()` mechanism: it centers the bubble on the element with no viewport-edge awareness, and the Inspiration badge sits in the header's literal top-right corner (per the earlier header-alignment fix) — the bubble overflowed off-screen, unreadable. Fixed in `wireTooltip()` itself (not special-cased for Inspiration), clamping the bubble's center so it can't render closer than 8px to either viewport edge. This is a shared fix — every `data-tip` element in the file benefits, not just Inspiration; confirmed via the math that it's a no-op for elements not near an edge (the clamp only engages when the unclamped position would overflow).
- **Sheet Conditions picker** (`sheet/index.html`, follow-up to the `dm.html` SCC fix below): the MainInfo header's Conditions row was free-text (type anything, generic purple pill, no tooltip) since the slice-1 header build — flagged then as a known gap ("no canonical color source"), no longer true now that the `conditions` Supabase table exists. Replaced with a picker: new `fetchConditionsData()` (`sb.from('conditions').select('*').order('sort_order')`, fetched once in the same init `Promise.all` as `fetchBackgroundData`/`fetchRegionData`/etc.) populates `CONDITIONS_DATA`; `renderConditions()` now renders a `<select>` of conditions not already applied instead of a text input, and each pill's colors come from real `color_bg`/`color_border`/`color_text` (CSS custom properties per pill, same `--cond-bg`/`--cond-border`/`--cond-text` technique `dm.html`'s SCC Live Session panel uses) with real `tip` text wired through the existing `wireAllTooltips()` system built earlier this session — conditions now get real hover tooltips for the first time. Removed the now-inaccurate CSS comment claiming conditions have no color source. Testing also surfaced (not introduced, not fixed) a latent race in the existing `saveSheet()`-per-click pattern: two `removeCondition()` calls fired with zero delay between them (only happened in my own test script, calling the function directly twice in a row) can let an earlier PATCH complete after a later one and leave stale data in Supabase. Confirmed real click-driven usage — any perceptible delay between actions — doesn't hit this, and the fire-and-forget `saveSheet()` pattern itself is unchanged from the old free-text flow, so this is a pre-existing risk, not something this edit introduced. Left as a noted risk rather than fixed unprompted.
- **SCC Live Session conditions — added "Muted"** (`dm.html`): `SCC_CONDITIONS_LIST` now has 17 entries (was 16). More importantly, fixed the underlying gap this surfaced — `sccLiveRenderConditions()`'s `COND_DATA` is a fully local, hardcoded object covering only the 15 core D&D conditions; "Fading Stage 1" and "Color-Drained" were already in the picker list but had no entry in `COND_DATA`, so they silently fell through to a generic purple placeholder with no real tooltip (just the condition's own name). Rather than add a third hardcoded copy of Muted's color/tip (a real `conditions` table already exists in Supabase, seeded same-day, `is_custom:true` for the 3 Anavale-specific ones), added a `conditions` fetch to `sccInit()`'s existing `Promise.all` (same pattern as `species`/`items`) into `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA`, and changed the lookup chain to `COND_DATA[c] || sccConditionsTableLookup(c) || genericFallback`. This fixes Muted **and** retroactively fixes Fading Stage 1/Color-Drained's missing real tooltips as a side effect of the same code path — not scope creep, the fix the DM asked for necessarily covered all three. Left the separate, unrelated `COND_DATA` in `sccPromptCondition()` (combat-tab "Add Condition" modal, line ~12421) untouched — different feature, not mentioned, out of scope. Verified: picker dropdown now lists Muted; added Muted + Fading Stage 1 + Poisoned to a test player, confirmed all three render with correct real colors/tooltips (Muted and Fading Stage 1 from Supabase, Poisoned still from local `COND_DATA` — correct precedence), screenshotted, then cleared the test state (`SCC_LIVE_CONDITIONS` is pure client-side in-memory state, confirmed via grep it's never written to Supabase, so no DB cleanup was needed).

## 3. Repo & Deployment

**Local path:** `Documents/DND/Anavale/anavale-wiki/`

    Documents/DND/Anavale/
    ├── anavale-wiki/          ← this repo (public git)
    ├── anavale-dm-docs/       ← private git repo (DM spoiler files)
    └── anavale-assets/        ← non-git large files (mockups, world art, etc.)

**GitHub repo:** `github.com/carlosknightiii/anavale-wiki`

**Live URLs:**
| URL | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki` | Player-facing wiki |
| `https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki/dm` | DM Tools (`anavale-dm`) |
| `https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki/character` | Character creator |

**Git push sequence:**

    cd Documents/DND/Anavale/anavale-wiki
    git add -A
    git commit -m "..."
    git push

**Pre-push syntax check (Node v24 — `node --check` does not work on .html files):**

    node -e "require('fs').readFileSync('dm.html','utf8')" 2>&1

Returns nothing = safe. Throws = fix before pushing.

**CDN propagation delay:** GitHub Pages can take 5–15 minutes after a successful push. Before concluding a fix didn't land:

    curl -s "https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki/dm.html" | grep -c "FUNCTION_OR_STRING"

Count 0 when it should be 1+ = CDN hasn't propagated. Wait 5 minutes, re-run. Never write a new fix based on browser behavior without confirming via curl first.

**Site crash recovery:**

    git log --oneline -10
    git reset --hard <last-known-good-hash> && git push origin main --force

Use `git reset --hard`, not chained `git revert`. Chained reverts create indeterminate state.

**Git lock error:** `rm -f .git/index.lock` before retrying. Never force-push to resolve a lock.

## 4. Architecture

**Code changes → git push.** HTML, CSS, JS files only.
**Content changes → Supabase only. Never git push for content.** The `data/*.js` files are legacy seeds — not the source of truth. Never read from them in new code.

**Key files:**
| File | Notes |
|---|---|
| `dm.html` | DM Tools + SCC. Most complex file. Always stale — Rule C1 applies. |
| `character.html` | Character creator shell. Potentially stale every session. |
| `css/character.css` | Character creator styles. Potentially stale. |
| `js/character.js` | Character creator logic. Potentially stale. |
| `sheet/index.html` | Player character sheet. Potentially stale. |
| `js/wiki.js` | Wiki render logic. |
| `js/router.js` | Client-side routing. |
| `css/tokens.css` | Single source of truth for all design tokens. No `:root` blocks anywhere else. |
| `index.html` | Player-facing wiki shell. |

**The five always-stale files:** `dm.html`, `character.html`, `css/character.css`, `js/character.js`, `sheet/index.html` — read the current file state before every edit. Never assume. Never use project knowledge as a substitute for reading these files.

## 6. Debugging Rules (D1–D7)

**D1 — 2-attempt hard limit (non-negotiable).** If a fix fails twice, STOP. Do not write a third variation. Something is wrong with the diagnosis, not the fix. Say: *"I have reached the 2-attempt limit. I will not write another fix until I have read the exact current state of the broken code and identified the specific line that is wrong."*

**D2 — Diagnose before touching anything.** Read the actual broken code. Identify the exact line that is wrong and state precisely why it produces the observed behaviour. If uncertain, say so.

**D3 — Fix in one shot.** Once the root cause is identified, write one targeted fix. Do not patch symptoms.

**D4 — "Stop. Think. One fix."** When the DM writes this: halt all iteration. Re-read relevant code from scratch. State the root cause in plain English. Write one fix. Do not explain previous attempts.

**D5 — "Rewrite from first principles."** When the DM writes this: discard all accumulated edits. Write a clean version from scratch.

**D6 — Never answer without evidence.** Before answering any question about the project — code, UI, file structure, feature behavior — read the file or search project knowledge first. If the answer cannot be confirmed, say "I don't know — let me check."

**D7 — When the DM corrects a factual claim:** Stop immediately. Do not defend the previous answer. Correct the record in the Decision Log before writing any code.

---

## 7. Site Integrity Rules (C1–C5)

**C1 — Read dm.html before every edit. No exceptions.** Read the current state of the target function with line numbers before writing any replacement. Project knowledge is never sufficient.

**C2 — No literal emoji in JS strings.** All emoji and special characters inside JS string literals in HTML files must use `&#decimal;` entities or `\uXXXX` escapes. A single literal emoji produces `Uncaught SyntaxError: Invalid or unexpected token at dm:1:1` — crashing the entire page with no useful error message.
- Safe: `&#128203;` · `—`
- Unsafe: literal 📋 or — inside a quoted JS string

**C3 — Pre-push confirmation for dm.html.** Before any dm.html push, show the diff and confirm: no literal emoji in JS strings, no unmatched quotes.

**C4 — Site crash recovery: `git reset --hard`, not `git revert` chains.** See Section 3.

**C5 — Rejected diagnosis is confirmed wrong. Stop completely.** If the DM says a diagnosis is wrong, stop all iteration. Read the actual current code state. State the new diagnosis with the specific line and reason. Write one fix only after the new diagnosis is confirmed.

---

## 8. Code Authoring Rules

**Claude Code prompts containing code blocks:**
Always use a bash heredoc when writing file contents that include backtick code blocks. Never wrap such a prompt in a fenced code block — inner backticks will break the outer fence and content will be silently dropped.

    cat > filename.md << 'ENDOFFILE'
    ...content with any backticks, code blocks, special characters...
    ENDOFFILE

**JS strings (all files):**
- Never use literal emoji in JS strings — use `&#decimal;` or `\uXXXX` (mirrors C2)
- When running sed or regex replacements, always show the diff before committing

**CSS (`character.css`, `tokens.css`):**
- Define `:root` tokens before any component rules
- No inline `style=` attributes — all styles go in character.css
- No raw `rgba()` or hex in rules — every color references a CSS variable (exceptions: `:root` definitions and canvas JS strings)
- Semantic variable naming: `--char-card-bg` ✓ · `--char-dark-blue` ✗
- Shared elements use shared classes — no one-off cosmetic classes
- No duplicate rules — edit the existing rule, never add a second copy
- Only add a new variable when the value appears in more than one rule

**Tooltips (`character.js`, `character.html`):**
- Always `data-tip`, never `title` — `title` triggers native browser tooltip
- `initTooltips()` is an empty stub — use `wireTooltip(el)` directly
- Static HTML wires automatically on DOMContentLoaded
- Dynamic content must call `wireTooltip(el)` per element after rendering

**Positioning (`character.html`):**
- Always `position: sticky` — never JS scroll listeners for sticky behaviour
- `body.char-page` has `overflow-x: hidden` which breaks `window.scrollY` in Chrome/Safari
- Before adding any `position:fixed` or `position:absolute;inset:0` element: confirm z-index, confirm `pointer-events:none` unless interaction required, confirm it will be removed from DOM after use

**z-index ladder:**
| Element | z-index |
|---|---|
| Tooltips | 99999 |
| Confirmation overlay | 200 |
| Return banner | 100 |
| Sticky HUD (Stage 4) | 90 |
| Sticky Stage 3 panel | 50 |

**Modals (`sheet/index.html`):**
- All modals must be injected into `#cs-modal-root` — a direct sibling of `<main id="cs-main">` inside `<body>`
- Never build modals as part of `renderCharacter()`'s HTML string
- Reason: `.cs-wrap` has a `cs-fadein` animation with `forwards` fill-mode — any `animation`, `transform`, `filter`, or `perspective` on an ancestor creates a new containing block for `position:fixed` descendants, breaking them silently. Symptom: modal appears at the top of the page regardless of scroll position.

**Canvas (`welcome.html`):**
- Partition animation text is drawn via `drawText(p)` — never via HTML/CSS
- Never add HTML elements inside `#partition-sticky`

## 9. Design Tokens

All tokens live in `css/tokens.css` — single source of truth. No `:root` blocks anywhere else.

**Fonts (two only):**
- `--font-display: 'Cinzel Decorative', Georgia, serif` — all headings, Bold 700 only
- `--font-body: 'Roboto', system-ui, sans-serif` — all body text, labels, UI (300/400/700)
- `--font-mono: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace` — Formery stamps, code

**Aliases:** `--font-headers` → `var(--font-display)` · `--font-serif` → `var(--font-display)` · `--font-sans` → `var(--font-body)`

**Gold:** `#f0b429` everywhere. Old `#c8940a` retired. Do not re-add Charm or EB Garamond.

**Google Fonts load:** `Cinzel+Decorative:wght@700 + Roboto:ital,wght@0,300;0,400;0,700;1,300;1,400;1,700`
